Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_DeleteById] 
    @TranslationId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if AdviceTranslationsId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE AdviceTranslations
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        TranslationId = @TranslationId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @TranslationId AS UpdatedTranslationId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedTranslationId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_GetAll]

AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        TranslationId,
        AdviceId,
        LanguageId,
        TranslatedAdvice,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    FROM AdviceTranslations
    WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
END;
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_GetById]
    @AdviceTranslationsId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        TranslationId,
        AdviceId,
        LanguageId,
        ISNULL(TranslatedAdvice, '') AS TranslatedAdvice,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    FROM AdviceTranslations
    WHERE TranslationId = @AdviceTranslationsId AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_Insert]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_Insert]
    @TranslationId INT,
    @AdviceId INT,
    @LanguageId INT,
    @TranslatedAdvice NVARCHAR(MAX),
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO AdviceTranslations(
        AdviceId,
        LanguageId,
        TranslatedAdvice,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @AdviceId,
        @LanguageId,
        @TranslatedAdvice,
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS AdviceTranslationsId;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[AdviceTranslations_Update] 
    @TranslationId INT,
    @AdviceId INT = NULL,
    @LanguageId INT = NULL,
    @TranslatedAdvice NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if AdviceTranslationsId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE AdviceTranslations
    SET 
        AdviceId = CASE WHEN @AdviceId > 0 THEN @AdviceId ELSE AdviceId END,
        LanguageId = CASE WHEN @LanguageId > 0 THEN @LanguageId ELSE LanguageId END,
        TranslatedAdvice = ISNULL(@TranslatedAdvice, TranslatedAdvice),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        TranslationId = @TranslationId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @TranslationId AS UpdatedAdviceTranslationsId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedAdviceTranslationsId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Appointment_DeleteById]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Appointment_DeleteById]
    @AppointmentId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    -- Soft delete: Update only if appointment exists and is not already deleted
    UPDATE [dbo].[Appointment]
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE Id = @AppointmentId AND IsDeleted = 0;
    
    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @AppointmentId AS DeletedAppointmentId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS DeletedAppointmentId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Appointment_GetAll]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Appointment_GetAll]
(
      @DoctorId INT = NULL,
      @SessionId INT = NULL,
      @ScheduleId INT = NULL,
      @SearchText NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
      @PageNumber INT = 1,
      @PageSize   INT = 10,
      @TotalCount INT OUTPUT
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- TOTAL COUNT
    --------------------------------------------------
    SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(*)
    FROM [dbo].[Appointment] a
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[Patients] p ON a.PatientId = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[Users] u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    WHERE 
        a.IsDeleted = 0
        AND (@DoctorId IS NULL OR a.DoctorProfileId = @DoctorId)
        AND (@SessionId IS NULL OR a.SessionId = @SessionId)
        AND (@ScheduleId IS NULL OR a.ScheduleId = @ScheduleId)
        AND (
                @SearchText IS NULL OR
                u.FirstName LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                u.LastName LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                u.PhoneNumber LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                p.PatientCode LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%'
            );

    --------------------------------------------------
    -- PAGINATED DATA
    --------------------------------------------------
    SELECT 
        a.Id AS AppointmentId,
        a.SessionId,
        a.ScheduleId,
        a.SerialNo,
        a.AppointmentDate,
        a.CreatedAt,
        a.UpdatedAt,
        
        p.PatientID,
        p.PatientCode,
        p.PatientAge,
        p.Gender,
        p.BloodGroup,

        u.UserID,
        u.FirstName AS PatientName,
        u.LastName,
        u.PhoneNumber
    FROM [dbo].[Appointment] a
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[Patients] p ON a.PatientId = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[Users] u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    WHERE 
        a.IsDeleted = 0
        AND (@DoctorId IS NULL OR a.DoctorProfileId = @DoctorId)
        AND (@SessionId IS NULL OR a.SessionId = @SessionId)
        AND (@ScheduleId IS NULL OR a.ScheduleId = @ScheduleId)
        AND (
                @SearchText IS NULL OR
                u.FirstName LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                u.LastName LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                u.PhoneNumber LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%' OR
                p.PatientCode LIKE '%' + @SearchText + '%'
            )
    ORDER BY a.Id DESC
    OFFSET ((@PageNumber - 1) * @PageSize) ROWS
    FETCH NEXT @PageSize ROWS ONLY;
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Appointment_GetById]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Appointment_GetById]
(
    @AppointmentId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        A.Id AS AppointmentId,
        A.SessionId,
        A.ScheduleId,
        A.AppointmentDate,
        A.SerialNo,
        A.DoctorProfileId,

        -- Patient Info
        P.PatientID ,
        P.DateOfBirth,
        P.Gender,
        P.Address,
        P.BloodGroup,
        P.InsuranceProvider,
        P.InsurancePolicyNumber,
        P.PatientAge,
        P.PatientCode,
        U.FirstName as PatientName,
        U.LastName,
        U.Email as PatientEmail,
        U.PhoneNumber 

    FROM Appointment A
    LEFT JOIN Patients P ON P.PatientID = A.PatientId
    LEFT JOIN Users U ON U.UserID = P.UserID

    WHERE A.Id = @AppointmentId
      AND A.IsDeleted = 0
      AND P.IsDeleted = 0
      AND U.IsDeleted = 0;

END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Appointment_Insert]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Appointment_Insert]
(
    @PatientName NVARCHAR(100),
    @PhoneNumber NVARCHAR(20),
    @Gender NVARCHAR(50),
    @BloodGroup NVARCHAR(10),
    @Age INT,
    @SessionId INT,
    @ScheduleId INT,
    @DoctorProfileId INT,
    @AppointmentDate DATETIME
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE 
        @NewUserID INT = NULL,
        @NewPatientID INT = NULL,
        @NewAppointmentID INT,
        @PatientCode VARCHAR(10),
        @SerialNo INT;

    BEGIN TRY
        BEGIN TRANSACTION;

        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 1ï¸âƒ£ CHECK EXISTING PATIENT BY PHONE NUMBER
        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        SELECT TOP 1
            @NewPatientID = p.PatientID,
            @NewUserID = u.UserID
        FROM [dbo].[Patients] p
        JOIN [dbo].[Users] u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
        WHERE u.PhoneNumber = @PhoneNumber;


        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 2ï¸âƒ£ IF NEW â†’ INSERT USER & PATIENT
        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        IF @NewPatientID IS NULL
        BEGIN
            -- Generate Patient Code
            SET @PatientCode = 'PA' + UPPER(SUBSTRING(REPLACE(NEWID(), '-', ''), 1, 6));

            -------------------------
            -- Insert into Users
            -------------------------
            INSERT INTO [dbo].[Users]
            (
                TenantID, FirstName, LastName, Email, PasswordHash, UserType,
                PhoneNumber, CreatedAt, UpdatedAt, IsActive, IsDeleted, ReferenceUserId
            )
            VALUES
            (
                1,
                @PatientName,
                @PatientName,
                'Patient@gmail.com',
                'Patient',
                'Patient',
                @PhoneNumber,
                GETUTCDATE(),
                GETUTCDATE(),
                1,
                0,
                NULL
            );

            SET @NewUserID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

            -------------------------
            -- Insert into Patients
            -------------------------
            INSERT INTO [dbo].[Patients]
            (
                UserID, DateOfBirth, Gender, Address, BloodGroup,
                InsuranceProvider, InsurancePolicyNumber, CreatedAt, UpdatedAt,
                IsDeleted, PatientReferenceID, PatientAge, PatientCode
            )
            VALUES
            (
                @NewUserID,
                NULL,
                @Gender,
                NULL,
                @BloodGroup,
                NULL,
                NULL,
                GETUTCDATE(),
                GETUTCDATE(),
                0,
                NULL,
                @Age,
                @PatientCode
            );

            SET @NewPatientID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
        END


        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 3ï¸âƒ£ GET NEXT SERIAL NUMBER
        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        SELECT @SerialNo = ISNULL(MAX(SerialNo), 0) + 1
        FROM [dbo].[Appointment]
        WHERE SessionId = @SessionId
          AND ScheduleId = @ScheduleId
          AND CAST(AppointmentDate AS DATE) = CAST(@AppointmentDate AS DATE);


        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 4ï¸âƒ£ INSERT APPOINTMENT
        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        INSERT INTO [dbo].[Appointment]
        (
            SessionId,
            ScheduleId,
            IsDeleted,
            CreatedAt,
            UpdatedAt,
            PatientId,
            AppointmentDate,
            SerialNo,
            DoctorProfileId
        )
        VALUES
        (
            @SessionId,
            @ScheduleId,
            0,
            GETUTCDATE(),
            GETUTCDATE(),
            @NewPatientID,
            @AppointmentDate,
            @SerialNo,
            @DoctorProfileId
        );

        SET @NewAppointmentID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();

        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        -- 5ï¸âƒ£ COMMIT + RETURN SERIAL NUMBER
        ----------------------------------------------------------------------
        COMMIT TRANSACTION;

        SELECT @SerialNo AS SerialNo;

    END TRY
    BEGIN CATCH
        IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0 ROLLBACK;

        SELECT ERROR_NUMBER() AS ErrorNumber, ERROR_MESSAGE() AS ErrorMessage;
    END CATCH
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Appointment_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Appointment_Update]
(
    @Id INT,
    @SessionId INT = NULL,
    @ScheduleId INT = NULL,
    @PatientId INT = NULL,
    @AppointmentDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @DoctorProfileId INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    -- Update only if appointment exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE [dbo].[Appointment]
    SET 
        SessionId = CASE WHEN @SessionId IS NOT NULL THEN @SessionId ELSE SessionId END,
        ScheduleId = CASE WHEN @ScheduleId IS NOT NULL THEN @ScheduleId ELSE ScheduleId END,
        PatientId = CASE WHEN @PatientId IS NOT NULL THEN @PatientId ELSE PatientId END,
        AppointmentDate = CASE WHEN @AppointmentDate IS NOT NULL THEN @AppointmentDate ELSE AppointmentDate END,
        DoctorProfileId = CASE WHEN @DoctorProfileId IS NOT NULL THEN @DoctorProfileId ELSE DoctorProfileId END,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE Id = @Id AND IsDeleted = 0;
    
    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @Id AS UpdatedAppointmentId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedAppointmentId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CampaignDoctor_Update]
    @CampaignDoctorID INT,
    @DoctorID INT,
    @CampaignID INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[CampaignDoctor]
    SET 
        [DoctorID] = @DoctorID,
        [CampaignID] = @CampaignID,
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE()
    WHERE [CampaignDoctorID] = @CampaignDoctorID
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0;
    SET @UpdatedId = @CampaignDoctorID;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CommonAdvices_GetByName]
    @AdviceName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [CommonAdviceID],
        [Advice],
        [Type],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted],
        [IsActive]
    FROM [dbo].[CommonAdvices]
    WHERE ([Advice] LIKE '%' + @AdviceName + '%' OR @AdviceName IS NULL)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[CommonHistory_GetByName]
    @CommonHistoryName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [CommonHistoryId],
        [Name],
        [Description],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted],
        [IsActive]
    FROM [dbo].[CommonHistory]
    WHERE ([Name] LIKE '%' + @CommonHistoryName + '%' OR @CommonHistoryName IS NULL)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

        [Name],

        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @Name,
        @Description,
        @IsActive,

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Degree_DeleteById] 
    @DegreeID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DegreeID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Degree
    SET 
		IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DegreeID = @DegreeID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DegreeID AS UpdatedDegreeID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDegreeID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Degree_GetById]
    @DegreeID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        DegreeID,
        TenantID,
        ISNULL(DegreeName, '') AS DegreeName,
	Duration,
        ISNULL(DurationType, '') AS DurationType,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    FROM Degree
    WHERE DegreeID = @DegreeID AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Degree_Insert]
    @DegreeID INT,
    @TenantId INT,
    @DegreeName NVARCHAR(100)=NULL,
    @Duration NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @DurationType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Degree(
        TenantId,
        DegreeName,
        Duration,
        DurationType,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantId,
		 ISNULL(@DegreeName, ''),
		 @Duration,
		 ISNULL(@DurationType, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS DegreeID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Degree_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Degree_Update] 
    @DegreeID INT,
    @TenantId INT = NULL,
    @DegreeName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Duration INT = NULL,
    @DurationType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    -- Update only if DegreeID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Degree
    SET 
        TenantId = CASE WHEN @TenantId > 0 THEN @TenantId ELSE TenantId END,
        DegreeName = ISNULL(@DegreeName, DegreeName),
        Duration = @Duration,
        DurationType = ISNULL(@DurationType, DurationType),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DegreeID = @DegreeID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DegreeID AS UpdatedDegreeID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDegreeID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Diagonosis_Insert]
    @DiagonosisID INT,
    @Name NVARCHAR(100),
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Code NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    INSERT INTO Diagonosis (
        Name,
        Description,
        Code,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
        IsActive
    )
    VALUES (
        @Name,
        ISNULL(@Description, ''),
        ISNULL(@Code, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0,  -- Default value for IsDeleted
        1   -- Default value for IsActive
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS DiagnosisID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Diagonosis_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Diagonosis_Update] 
    @DiagonosisID INT,
    @Name NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@CreateAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@Code NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DiagonosisID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Diagonosis
    SET 
        Name = ISNULL(@Name, Name),                -- Update Name if provided
		Code=ISNULL(@Code,Code),
        Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),  -- Update Description if provided
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE(),                   -- Set UpdatedAt to current UTC time
        IsDeleted = ISNULL(@IsDeleted, IsDeleted),   -- Update IsDeleted if provided
        IsActive = ISNULL(@IsActive, IsActive)      -- Update IsActive if provided
    WHERE 
        DiagonosisID = @DiagonosisID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;                         -- Ensure the record is not deleted

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DiagonosisID AS UpdatedDiagonosisID;  -- Return the updated DiagonosisID
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDiagonosisID;  -- Return NULL if no update occurred
    END
END;
GO

    @DiseaseID INT,
    @TenantId INT = NULL,
    @DiseaseName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DiseaseID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Diseases
    SET 
        TenantId = CASE WHEN @TenantId > 0 THEN @TenantId ELSE TenantId END,
        DiseaseName = ISNULL(@DiseaseName, DiseaseName),
        Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DiseaseId = @DiseaseID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DiseaseID AS UpdatedDiseaseID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDiseaseID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[District_GetAll]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[District_GetAll]
(
    @DivisionId INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    BEGIN TRY
        SELECT 
            DistrictId,
            DistrictName,
            DivisionId
        FROM dbo.Districts
        WHERE 
            (@DivisionId IS NULL OR DivisionId = @DivisionId)
        ORDER BY 
            DistrictName;
    END TRY
    BEGIN CATCH
        DECLARE @ErrorMessage NVARCHAR(4000) = ERROR_MESSAGE();
        DECLARE @ErrorSeverity INT = ERROR_SEVERITY();
        DECLARE @ErrorState INT = ERROR_STATE();

        RAISERROR (@ErrorMessage, @ErrorSeverity, @ErrorState);
    END CATCH
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Division_GetAll]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Division_GetAll]
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        DivisionID,
        DivisionName
    FROM 
        Divisions
    ORDER BY 
        DivisionName ASC;
END
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Doctor_Insert]
    @DoctorID INT,
    @UserID INT,
    @Specialization NVARCHAR(100),
    @LicenseNumber NVARCHAR(50),
	@DoctorReferenceID INT = NULL,
    @HospitalAffiliation NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Expertise NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @ProfileStep INT = 0, -- Default to 0 for new doctors (onboarding not completed)
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Doctors(
        UserID,
		DoctorReferenceID,
        Specialization,
        LicenseNumber,
        HospitalAffiliation,
        Expertise,
        ProfileStep,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @UserID,
		@DoctorReferenceID,
       ISNULL( @Specialization, ''),
		ISNULL(@LicenseNumber, ''),
        ISNULL(@HospitalAffiliation, ''),
        @Expertise,
        ISNULL(@ProfileStep, 0), -- Set ProfileStep to 0 for new doctors (onboarding not completed)
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS DoctorID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Doctor_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Doctor_Update] 
    @DoctorID INT,
    @UserID INT = NULL,
    @Specialization NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @LicenseNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@DoctorReferenceID INT=NULL,
    @HospitalAffiliation NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Expertise NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @ProfileStep INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DoctorID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Doctors
    SET 
        UserID = CASE WHEN @UserID > 0 THEN @UserID ELSE UserID END,
		DoctorReferenceID = CASE WHEN @DoctorReferenceID > 0 THEN @DoctorReferenceID ELSE DoctorReferenceID END,
        Specialization = ISNULL(@Specialization, Specialization),
        LicenseNumber = ISNULL(@LicenseNumber, LicenseNumber),
        HospitalAffiliation = ISNULL(@HospitalAffiliation, HospitalAffiliation),
        Expertise = ISNULL(@Expertise, Expertise),
        ProfileStep = ISNULL(@ProfileStep, ProfileStep),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DoctorID = @DoctorID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DoctorID AS UpdatedDoctorID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDoctorID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorChamber_GetByDoctorId]
    @DoctorID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [ChamberID],
        [TenantID],
        [DoctorID],
        [ChamberName],
        [Address],
        [Country],
        [CountryID],
        [City],
        [CityID],
        [ZipCode],
        [ZipCodeID],
        [IsVisibleOnPrescription],
        [ChamberReferenceId],
        [DistrictId],
        [DivisionId],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[DoctorChamber]
    WHERE [DoctorID] = @DoctorID
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorChamber_Insert]
    @ChamberID INT,
    @TenantID INT,
    @DoctorID INT,
    @ChamberName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Address NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Country NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CountryID INT = NULL,
    @City NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CityID INT = NULL,
    @ZipCode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @ZipCodeID INT = NULL,
    @IsVisibleOnPrescription BIT = 1,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = 0
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO DoctorChambers (
        TenantID,
        DoctorID,
        ChamberName,
        Address,
        Country,
        CountryID,
        City,
        CityID,
        ZipCode,
        ZipCodeID,
        IsVisibleOnPrescription,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantID,
        @DoctorID,
        ISNULL(@ChamberName, ''),
        ISNULL(@Address, ''),
        ISNULL(@Country, ''),
        @CountryID,
        ISNULL(@City, ''),
        @CityID,
        ISNULL(@ZipCode, ''),
        @ZipCodeID,
        @IsVisibleOnPrescription,
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        @IsDeleted
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ChamberID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS ChamberID;
END;
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorChamber_Update] 
    @ChamberID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
    @ChamberName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Address NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Country NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CountryID INT = NULL,
    @City NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CityID INT = NULL,
    @ZipCode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @ZipCodeID INT = NULL,
    @IsVisibleOnPrescription BIT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ChamberID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE DoctorChambers
    SET 
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        DoctorID = CASE WHEN @DoctorID > 0 THEN @DoctorID ELSE DoctorID END,
        ChamberName = ISNULL(@ChamberName, ChamberName),
        Address = ISNULL(@Address, Address),
        Country = ISNULL(@Country, Country),
        CountryID = @CountryID,
        City = ISNULL(@City, City),
        CityID = @CityID,
        ZipCode = ISNULL(@ZipCode, ZipCode),
        ZipCodeID = @ZipCodeID,
        IsVisibleOnPrescription = COALESCE(@IsVisibleOnPrescription, IsVisibleOnPrescription),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ChamberID = @ChamberID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ChamberID AS UpdatedChamberID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedChamberID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorDegree_Update] 
    @DoctorDegreeID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
    @DegreeID INT = NULL,
    @PassingYear INT = NULL,
    @InstituteName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @InstituteID INT = NULL,
    @Country NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CountryID INT = NULL,
    @City NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CityID INT = NULL,
    @ZipCode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @ZipCodeID INT = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DoctorDegreeID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE DoctorDegree
    SET 
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        DoctorID = CASE WHEN @DoctorID > 0 THEN @DoctorID ELSE DoctorID END,
        DegreeID = CASE WHEN @DegreeID > 0 THEN @DegreeID ELSE DegreeID END,
        PassingYear = ISNULL(@PassingYear, PassingYear),
        InstituteName = ISNULL(@InstituteName, InstituteName),
        InstituteID = ISNULL(@InstituteID, InstituteID),
        Country = ISNULL(@Country, Country),
        CountryID = ISNULL(@CountryID, CountryID),
        City = ISNULL(@City, City),
        CityID = ISNULL(@CityID, CityID),
        ZipCode = ISNULL(@ZipCode, ZipCode),
        ZipCodeID = ISNULL(@ZipCodeID, ZipCodeID),
        IsDeleted = ISNULL(@IsDeleted, IsDeleted),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DoctorDegreeID = @DoctorDegreeID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DoctorDegreeID AS UpdatedDoctorDegreeID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDoctorDegreeID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;

    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO DoctorExpertise (
        TenantID,
        DoctorID,
        ExpertiseID,
        ExperienceYears,
        Certification,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantID,
        @DoctorID,
        @ExpertiseID,
        @ExperienceYears,
        ISNULL(@Certification, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS DoctorExpertiseID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[DoctorExpertise_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorExpertise_Update] 
    @DoctorExpertiseID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
    @ExpertiseID INT = NULL,
    @ExperienceYears INT = NULL,
    @Certification NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DoctorExpertiseID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE DoctorExpertise
    SET 
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        DoctorID = CASE WHEN @DoctorID > 0 THEN @DoctorID ELSE DoctorID END,
        ExpertiseID = CASE WHEN @ExpertiseID > 0 THEN @ExpertiseID ELSE ExpertiseID END,
        ExperienceYears = ISNULL(@ExperienceYears, ExperienceYears),
        Certification = ISNULL(@Certification, Certification),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DoctorExpertiseID = @DoctorExpertiseID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DoctorExpertiseID AS UpdatedDoctorExpertiseID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDoctorExpertiseID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorFeesSetup_GetByDoctorId]
    @DoctorID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        fs.[DoctorFeesSetupID],
        fs.[DoctorScheduleID],
        fs.[AppointmentType],
        fs.[CurrentFee],
        fs.[PreviousFee],
        fs.[FeeAppliedFrom],
        fs.[FollowUpPeriod],
        fs.[ReportShowPeriod],
        fs.[Discount],
        fs.[DiscountAppliedFrom],
        fs.[DiscountPeriod],
        fs.[TotalFee],
        fs.[IsActive],
        fs.[CreatedAt],
        fs.[UpdatedAt],
        fs.[IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[DoctorFeesSetup] fs
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[DoctorSchedule] ds ON fs.[DoctorScheduleID] = ds.[ScheduleID]
    WHERE ds.[DoctorID] = @DoctorID
        AND fs.[IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY fs.[CreatedAt] DESC;
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorFeesSetup_Insert]
    @DoctorScheduleID INT,
    @AppointmentType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CurrentFee DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @PreviousFee DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @FeeAppliedFrom DATETIME = NULL,
    @FollowUpPeriod INT = NULL,
    @ReportShowPeriod INT = NULL,
    @Discount DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @DiscountAppliedFrom DATETIME = NULL,
    @DiscountPeriod INT = NULL,
    @TotalFee DECIMAL(18, 2) = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @DoctorFeesSetupID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    INSERT INTO [dbo].[DoctorFeesSetup]
    (
        [DoctorScheduleID],
        [AppointmentType],
        [CurrentFee],
        [PreviousFee],
        [FeeAppliedFrom],
        [FollowUpPeriod],
        [ReportShowPeriod],
        [Discount],
        [DiscountAppliedFrom],
        [DiscountPeriod],
        [TotalFee],
        [IsActive],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @DoctorScheduleID,
        @AppointmentType,
        @CurrentFee,
        @PreviousFee,
        @FeeAppliedFrom,
        @FollowUpPeriod,
        @ReportShowPeriod,
        @Discount,
        @DiscountAppliedFrom,
        @DiscountPeriod,
        @TotalFee,
        @IsActive,
        GETDATE(),
        GETDATE(),
        0
    );
    SET @DoctorFeesSetupID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
END
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[DoctorSchedule_Update] 
    @DoctorScheduleID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
    @ScheduleID INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if DoctorScheduleID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE DoctorSchedule
    SET 
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        DoctorID = CASE WHEN @DoctorID > 0 THEN @DoctorID ELSE DoctorID END,
        ScheduleID = CASE WHEN @ScheduleID > 0 THEN @ScheduleID ELSE ScheduleID END,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        DoctorScheduleID = @DoctorScheduleID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @DoctorScheduleID AS UpdatedDoctorScheduleID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedDoctorScheduleID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_GetByEntityIdAndType]
    @EntityId INT,
    @EntityType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @AttachmentType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @RelatedEntityid INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [DocumentsAttachmentID],
        [FileName],
        [OriginalFileName],
        [Path],
        [EntityType],
        [EntityId],
        [AttachmentType],
        [RelatedEntityid],
        [TenantID],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment]
    WHERE [EntityId] = @EntityId
        AND (@EntityType IS NULL OR [EntityType] = @EntityType)
        AND (@AttachmentType IS NULL OR [AttachmentType] = @AttachmentType)
        AND (@RelatedEntityid IS NULL OR [RelatedEntityid] = @RelatedEntityid)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_GetDocumentInfo]
    @EntityId INT,
    @EntityType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @AttachmentType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT TOP 1
        [DocumentsAttachmentID],
        [FileName],
        [OriginalFileName],
        [Path],
        [EntityType],
        [EntityId],
        [AttachmentType],
        [RelatedEntityid],
        [TenantID],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment]
    WHERE [EntityId] = @EntityId
        AND (@EntityType IS NULL OR [EntityType] = @EntityType)
        AND (@AttachmentType IS NULL OR [AttachmentType] = @AttachmentType)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_GetPaginated]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_GetPaginated]
    @Sorting NVARCHAR(100) = '',
    @SkipCount INT = 0,
    @MaxResultCount INT = 10
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    DECLARE @SQL NVARCHAR(MAX);
    SET @SQL = N'
    SELECT 
        [DocumentsAttachmentID],
        [FileName],
        [OriginalFileName],
        [Path],
        [EntityType],
        [EntityId],
        [AttachmentType],
        [RelatedEntityid],
        [TenantID],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment]
    WHERE [IsDeleted] = 0';
    
    IF @Sorting IS NOT NULL AND @Sorting != ''
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = @SQL + N' ORDER BY ' + @Sorting;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SET @SQL = @SQL + N' ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC';
    END
    
    SET @SQL = @SQL + N' OFFSET ' + CAST(@SkipCount AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N' ROWS FETCH NEXT ' + CAST(@MaxResultCount AS NVARCHAR(10)) + N' ROWS ONLY';
    
    EXEC sp_executesql @SQL;
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_Insert]
    @FileName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @OriginalFileName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @Path NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
    @EntityType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @EntityId INT = NULL,
    @AttachmentType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @RelatedEntityid INT = NULL,
    @TenantID INT,
    @DocumentsAttachmentID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN

    INSERT INTO [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment]
    (
        [FileName],
        [OriginalFileName],

        [EntityType],

        [IsDeleted]
    )

    (

        @OriginalFileName,
        @Path,

        @RelatedEntityid,
        @TenantID,

        0
    );

END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment_Update]
    @DocumentsAttachmentID INT,
    @FileName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @OriginalFileName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @Path NVARCHAR(1000) = NULL,
    @EntityType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @EntityId INT = NULL,
    @AttachmentType NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @RelatedEntityid INT = NULL,
    @TenantID INT,
    @UpdatedDocumentsAttachmentID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[DocumentsAttachment]
    SET 
        [FileName] = @FileName,
        [OriginalFileName] = @OriginalFileName,
        [Path] = @Path,
        [EntityType] = @EntityType,
        [EntityId] = @EntityId,
        [AttachmentType] = @AttachmentType,
        [RelatedEntityid] = @RelatedEntityid,
        [TenantID] = @TenantID,
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE()
    WHERE [DocumentsAttachmentID] = @DocumentsAttachmentID
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0;
    
    SET @UpdatedDocumentsAttachmentID = @DocumentsAttachmentID;
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[EmailTemplate_GetById]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[EmailTemplate_GetById]
    @EmailTemplateId UNIQUEIDENTIFIER
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        Id,
        Header,
        ISNULL(Description, '') AS Description,
        ISNULL(Body, '') AS Body,
        ISNULL(Footer, '') AS Footer,
        email_template_type AS EmailTemplateType,
        ISNULL(attachment_html_code, '') AS AttachmentHtmlCode
    FROM EmailTemplate
    WHERE Id = @EmailTemplateId 
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[EmailTemplate_Insert]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[EmailTemplate_Insert]
    @Id UNIQUEIDENTIFIER OUTPUT,
    @Header NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @Body NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @Footer NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @EmailTemplateType INT,
    @AttachmentHtmlCode NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Ensure the @Id value is passed into the procedure
    IF @Id IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SET @Id = NEWID();  -- Generate a new GUID if it's NULL
    END

    -- Insert the email template into the table
    INSERT INTO EmailTemplate (
        Id, 
        Header, 
        Description, 
        Body, 
        Footer, 
        email_template_type, 
        attachment_html_code
    )
    VALUES (
        @Id,  -- Use the GUID passed into this parameter
        ISNULL(@Header, ''), 
        ISNULL(@Description, ''), 
        ISNULL(@Body, ''), 
        ISNULL(@Footer, ''), 
        @EmailTemplateType, 
        ISNULL(@AttachmentHtmlCode, '')
    );

    -- The @Id will be passed back as OUTPUT, no need to generate it again
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[EmailTemplate_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[EmailTemplate_Update]
    @Id UNIQUEIDENTIFIER OUTPUT,
    @Header NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @Body NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @Footer NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @EmailTemplateType INT = NULL,
    @AttachmentHtmlCode NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if EmailTemplateId exists
    UPDATE EmailTemplate
    SET 
        Header = ISNULL(@Header, Header),  -- Update Header if provided
        Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),  -- Update Description if provided
        Body = ISNULL(@Body, Body),        -- Update Body if provided
        Footer = ISNULL(@Footer, Footer),  -- Update Footer if provided
        email_template_type = ISNULL(@EmailTemplateType, email_template_type),  -- Update EmailTemplateType if provided
        attachment_html_code = ISNULL(@AttachmentHtmlCode, attachment_html_code)  -- Update AttachmentHtmlCode if provided
    WHERE 
        Id = @Id;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @Id AS UpdatedEmailTemplateID;  -- Return the updated EmailTemplateId
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedEmailTemplateID;  -- Return NULL if no update occurred
    END
END;
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Examinations_DeleteById] 
    @ExaminationID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ExaminationID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Examinations
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ExaminationId = @ExaminationID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ExaminationID AS UpdatedExaminationID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedExaminationID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Examinations_Insert]
    @ExaminationID INT,
    @TenantID INT,
    @PatientID INT,
    @DoctorID INT,
    @ExaminationDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @Findings NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
    @Notes NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@BloodPressure NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@Pulse NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@Temperature NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Examinations(
        TenantID,
        PatientID,
        DoctorID,
        ExaminationDate,
        Findings,
        Notes,
		BloodPressure,
		Pulse,
		Temperature,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantID,
        @PatientID,
        @DoctorID,
        ISNULL(@ExaminationDate, GETUTCDATE()),
        ISNULL(@Findings, ''),
        ISNULL(@Notes, ''),
		ISNULL(@BloodPressure,''),
		ISNULL(@Pulse,''),
		ISNULL(@Temperature,''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS ExaminationID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Examinations_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Examinations_Update] 
    @ExaminationId INT,
    @TenantId INT = NULL,
    @PatientId INT = NULL,
    @DoctorId INT = NULL,
    @ExaminationDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @Findings NVARCHAR(4000) = NULL,
    @Notes NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ExaminationId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Examinations
    SET 
        TenantId = CASE WHEN @TenantId > 0 THEN @TenantId ELSE TenantId END,
        PatientId = CASE WHEN @PatientId > 0 THEN @PatientId ELSE PatientId END,
        DoctorId = CASE WHEN @DoctorId > 0 THEN @DoctorId ELSE DoctorId END,
        ExaminationDate = ISNULL(@ExaminationDate, ExaminationDate),
        Findings = ISNULL(@Findings, Findings),
        Notes = ISNULL(@Notes, Notes),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ExaminationId = @ExaminationId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ExaminationId AS UpdatedExaminationId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedExaminationId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

AS

        [PatientFollowUpID] AS [FollowUpId],

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[GetMedicationByName]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[GetMedicationByName]
    @MedicationName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @MedicationName IS NULL
    BEGIN
        SELECT TOP 100 
            MedicationId,
            TenantId,
            MedicationBrandId,
            GenericName,
            DAR,
            CASE 
                WHEN ISNULL(DosageForm, '') <> '' 
                    THEN CONCAT(LEFT(DosageForm, 3), '. ', MedicationName, ' ', Strength)
                ELSE CONCAT(MedicationName, ' ', Strength)
            END AS MedicationName,
            Description,
            Manufacturer,
            Indication,
            DosageForm,
            Strength,
            CreatedAt,
            UpdatedAt,
            IsDeleted,
            IsActive
        FROM [dbo].[Medications]
        WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT TOP 100 
            MedicationId,
            TenantId,
            MedicationBrandId,
            GenericName,
            DAR,
            CASE 
                WHEN ISNULL(DosageForm, '') <> '' 
                    THEN CONCAT(LEFT(DosageForm, 3), '. ', MedicationName)
                ELSE CONCAT(MedicationName, ' ', Strength)
            END AS MedicationName,
            Description,
            Manufacturer,
            Indication,
            DosageForm,
            Strength,
            CreatedAt,
            UpdatedAt,
            IsDeleted,
            IsActive
        FROM [dbo].[Medications]
        WHERE IsDeleted = 0
         AND (
        -- Medication name (prefix search only)
        MedicationName LIKE @MedicationName + '%'

        -- Generic name
        OR GenericName LIKE '%' + @MedicationName + '%'

        -- Manufacturer
        OR Manufacturer LIKE '%' + @MedicationName + '%'

        -- Indication
        OR Indication LIKE '%' + @MedicationName + '%'
    )

    END
END
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[InsertMedication]
	@MedicationID INT OUTPUT,
    @TenantId INT,
    @MedicationName NVARCHAR(100),
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Manufacturer NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DosageForm NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Strength NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Medications(
        TenantId,
        MedicationName,
        Description,
        Manufacturer,
        DosageForm,
        Strength,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantId,
        @MedicationName,
        ISNULL(@Description, ''),
        ISNULL(@Manufacturer, ''),
        ISNULL(@DosageForm, ''),
        ISNULL(@Strength, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Investigation_GetInvestigationsByName]
    @InvestigationName NVARCHAR(500) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [InvestigationID],
        [Name],
        [Description],
        [Code],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted],
        [IsActive]
    FROM [dbo].[Investigation]
    WHERE ([Name] LIKE '%' + @InvestigationName + '%' OR @InvestigationName IS NULL)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Investigation_Insert]
    @Name NVARCHAR(500),
    @Description NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @Code NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @InvestigationID INT OUTPUT
AS

        [Name],

        [Code],

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Investigations_Insert]
    @InvestigationId INT,
    @Name NVARCHAR(100),
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@Code NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
	@IsActive BIT=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Investigations(
        Name,
        Description,
		Code,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
		IsActive
    )
    VALUES (
        @Name,
        ISNULL(@Description, ''),
		ISNULL(@Code, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0,
		1
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS InvestigationId;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Investigations_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Investigations_Update] 
    @InvestigationID INT,
    @Name NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@Code NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@IsActive BIT= NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if InvestigationID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Investigations
    SET 
        Name = ISNULL(@Name, Name),
        Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),
		Code = ISNULL(@Code, Code),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        InvestigationId = @InvestigationID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @InvestigationID AS UpdatedInvestigationID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedInvestigationID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_GetByName]
    @MedicationName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [MedicationId],
        [TenantId],
        [MedicationBrandId],
        [GenericName],
        [DAR],
        [MedicationName],
        [Description],
        [Manufacturer],
        [DosageForm],
        [Strength],
        [Indication],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted],
        [IsActive]
    FROM [dbo].[Medications]
    WHERE ([MedicationName] LIKE '%' + @MedicationName + '%' OR @MedicationName IS NULL)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_GetManufacturers]
    @ManufacturerName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT DISTINCT 
        m.Manufacturer AS Manufacturer
    FROM Medications m
    WHERE m.IsDeleted = 0
          AND m.Manufacturer IS NOT NULL
          AND (@ManufacturerName IS NULL OR m.Manufacturer LIKE '%' + @ManufacturerName + '%')
    ORDER BY m.Manufacturer;
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Medication_GetMostUsed]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_GetMostUsed]
    @PageNumber INT = 1,
    @PageSize INT = 10,
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CompanyName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DateFilter NVARCHAR(20) = NULL -- 'today', 'weekly', 'monthly', or NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @StartDate DATETIME = NULL;
    DECLARE @EndDate DATETIME = NULL;

    -- ðŸ•“ Determine date range
    IF @DateFilter = 'today'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'weekly'
    BEGIN
        -- Force Monday as start of week (regardless of DATEFIRST)
        SET @StartDate = DATEADD(DAY, 
                            - (DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()) + @@DATEFIRST - 2) % 7, 
                            CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                        );
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 7, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'monthly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(GETDATE()), MONTH(GETDATE()), 1);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(MONTH, 1, @StartDate);
    END

    ;WITH MedicationUsage AS
    (
        SELECT 
            m.MedicationID,
            m.MedicationName,
            m.Manufacturer,
            m.GenericName,
            COUNT(pi.PrescriptionItemID) AS UsageCount
        FROM Medications m
        INNER JOIN PrescriptionItems pi ON m.MedicationID = pi.MedicationID
        INNER JOIN Prescriptions p ON pi.PrescriptionID = p.PrescriptionID
        WHERE 
            m.IsDeleted = 0
            AND pi.IsDeleted = 0
            AND p.IsDeleted = 0

            -- ðŸ§© Company filter
            AND (
                @CompanyName IS NULL 
                OR m.Manufacturer = @CompanyName
            )

            -- ðŸ§© Search filter
            AND (
                @SearchTerm IS NULL
                OR m.MedicationName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR m.GenericName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            )

            -- ðŸ§© Date filter (unified)
            AND (
                @DateFilter IS NULL
                OR (p.CreatedAt >= @StartDate AND p.CreatedAt < @EndDate)
            )
        GROUP BY 
            m.MedicationID, m.MedicationName, m.Manufacturer, m.GenericName
    )
    SELECT 
        MedicationID,
        MedicationName,
        Manufacturer,
        GenericName,
        UsageCount
    FROM MedicationUsage
    ORDER BY UsageCount DESC
    OFFSET (@PageNumber - 1) * @PageSize ROWS
    FETCH NEXT @PageSize ROWS ONLY;
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Medication_GetMostUsed_Combined]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_GetMostUsed_Combined]
    @PageNumber INT = 1,
    @PageSize INT = 10,
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CompanyName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DateFilter NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    -- ðŸ”‘ New Output Parameter for Total Count
    @TotalCount INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @StartDate DATETIME = NULL;
    DECLARE @EndDate DATETIME = NULL;

    -- ðŸ“… Date filter logic (Same)
    IF @DateFilter = 'today'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'weekly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEADD(
                              DAY,
                              - (DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()) + @@DATEFIRST - 2) % 7,
                              CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                             );
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 7, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'monthly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(GETDATE()), MONTH(GETDATE()), 1);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(MONTH, 1, @StartDate);
    END;

    -- ðŸ§© Temp table
    IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#MedicationUsage') IS NOT NULL
        DROP TABLE #MedicationUsage;

    SELECT 
        m.MedicationID,
        m.MedicationName,
        m.Manufacturer,
        m.GenericName,
        COUNT(pi.PrescriptionItemID) AS UsageCount
    INTO #MedicationUsage
    FROM Medications m
    INNER JOIN PrescriptionItems pi ON m.MedicationID = pi.MedicationID
    INNER JOIN Prescriptions p ON pi.PrescriptionID = p.PrescriptionID
    WHERE 
        m.IsDeleted = 0
        AND pi.IsDeleted = 0
        AND p.IsDeleted = 0
        AND (@CompanyName IS NULL OR m.Manufacturer = @CompanyName)
        AND (
            @SearchTerm IS NULL
            OR m.MedicationName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR m.GenericName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
        )
        AND (
            @DateFilter IS NULL
            OR (p.CreatedAt >= @StartDate AND p.CreatedAt < @EndDate)
        )
    GROUP BY 
        m.MedicationID, m.MedicationName, m.Manufacturer, m.GenericName;

    -- ðŸ”‘ Assign Total Count to OUTPUT Parameter
    SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(*) FROM #MedicationUsage;

    -- ðŸ§¾ Only ONE result set: Paged data
    SELECT 
        MedicationID,
        MedicationName,
        Manufacturer,
        GenericName,
        UsageCount
    FROM #MedicationUsage
    ORDER BY UsageCount DESC
    OFFSET (@PageNumber - 1) * @PageSize ROWS
    FETCH NEXT @PageSize ROWS ONLY;

    DROP TABLE #MedicationUsage;
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_GetMostUsed_TotalCount]
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CompanyName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DateFilter NVARCHAR(20) = NULL -- 'today', 'weekly', 'monthly', or NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @StartDate DATETIME = NULL;
    DECLARE @EndDate DATETIME = NULL;

    -- ðŸ•“ Determine date range
    IF @DateFilter = 'today'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'weekly'
    BEGIN
        -- Force Monday as start of week (independent of DATEFIRST)
        SET @StartDate = DATEADD(
                            DAY, 
                            - (DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()) + @@DATEFIRST - 2) % 7,
                            CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                         );
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 7, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'monthly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(GETDATE()), MONTH(GETDATE()), 1);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(MONTH, 1, @StartDate);
    END

    ;WITH MedicationUsage AS
    (
        SELECT 
            m.MedicationID,
            m.MedicationName,
            m.Manufacturer,
            m.GenericName,
            COUNT(pi.PrescriptionItemID) AS UsageCount
        FROM Medications m
        INNER JOIN PrescriptionItems pi 
            ON m.MedicationID = pi.MedicationID
        INNER JOIN Prescriptions p 
            ON pi.PrescriptionID = p.PrescriptionID
        WHERE 
            m.IsDeleted = 0
            AND pi.IsDeleted = 0
            AND p.IsDeleted = 0

            -- ðŸ§© Company filter
            AND (
                @CompanyName IS NULL 
                OR m.Manufacturer = @CompanyName
            )

            -- ðŸ§© Search filter
            AND (
                @SearchTerm IS NULL
                OR m.MedicationName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR m.GenericName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            )

            -- ðŸ§© Date filter (applied only if @StartDate is not null)
            AND (
                @DateFilter IS NULL
                OR (p.CreatedAt >= @StartDate AND p.CreatedAt < @EndDate)
            )

        GROUP BY 
            m.MedicationID, m.MedicationName, m.Manufacturer, m.GenericName
    )
    SELECT COUNT(*) AS TotalCount
    FROM MedicationUsage;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_Insert]
    @TenantId INT,
    @MedicationBrandId INT,
    @GenericName NVARCHAR(255),
    @DAR NVARCHAR(50),
    @MedicationName NVARCHAR(100),
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Manufacturer NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DosageForm NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Strength NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Indication NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @MedicationId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    INSERT INTO [dbo].[Medications]
    (
        [TenantID],
        [MedicationBrandId],
        [GenericName],
        [DAR],
        [MedicationName],
        [Description],
        [Manufacturer],
        [DosageForm],
        [Strength],
        [Indication],
        [IsActive],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @TenantId,
        @MedicationBrandId,
        @GenericName,
        @DAR,
        @MedicationName,
        @Description,
        @Manufacturer,
        @DosageForm,
        @Strength,
        @Indication,
        @IsActive,
        GETDATE(),
        GETDATE(),
        0
    );
    SET @MedicationId = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Medication_Update]
    @MedicationId INT,
    @TenantId INT,
    @MedicationBrandId INT,
    @GenericName NVARCHAR(255),
    @DAR NVARCHAR(50),
    @MedicationName NVARCHAR(100),
    @Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Manufacturer NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DosageForm NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Strength NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Indication NVARCHAR(MAX) = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @UpdatedId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[Medications]
    SET 
        [TenantID] = @TenantId,
        [MedicationBrandId] = @MedicationBrandId,
        [GenericName] = @GenericName,
        [DAR] = @DAR,
        [MedicationName] = @MedicationName,
        [Description] = @Description,
        [Manufacturer] = @Manufacturer,
        [DosageForm] = @DosageForm,
        [Strength] = @Strength,
        [Indication] = @Indication,
        [IsActive] = @IsActive,
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE()
    WHERE [MedicationId] = @MedicationId
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0;
    SET @UpdatedId = @MedicationId;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MedicationBrand_DeleteById] 
    @MedicationBrandID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if MedicationBrandID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE MedicationBrands
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        MedicationBrandID = @MedicationBrandID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @MedicationBrandID AS UpdatedMedicationBrandID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedMedicationBrandID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MedicationBrand_Update] 
    @MedicationBrandId INT,
    @ManufacturerId INT = NULL,
    @BrandName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@IsActive BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if MedicationBrandId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE MedicationBrands
    SET 
        ManufacturerId = CASE WHEN @ManufacturerId > 0 THEN @ManufacturerId ELSE ManufacturerId END,
        BrandName = ISNULL(@BrandName, BrandName),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        MedicationBrandId = @MedicationBrandId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @MedicationBrandId AS UpdatedMedicationBrandId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedMedicationBrandId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[MedicationManufacturer_GetMostUsed_Combined]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[MedicationManufacturer_GetMostUsed_Combined]
    @PageNumber INT = 1,
    @PageSize INT = 10,
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CompanyName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @DateFilter NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @TotalCount INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @StartDate DATETIME = NULL;
    DECLARE @EndDate DATETIME = NULL;

    -- Date Filters
    IF @DateFilter = 'today'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 1, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'weekly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEADD(
                              DAY,
                              - (DATEPART(WEEKDAY, GETDATE()) + @@DATEFIRST - 2) % 7,
                              CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE)
                           );
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(DAY, 7, @StartDate);
    END
    ELSE IF @DateFilter = 'monthly'
    BEGIN
        SET @StartDate = DATEFROMPARTS(YEAR(GETDATE()), MONTH(GETDATE()), 1);
        SET @EndDate = DATEADD(MONTH, 1, @StartDate);
    END;

    -- Drop temp table if exists
    IF OBJECT_ID('tempdb..#ManufacturerUsage') IS NOT NULL
        DROP TABLE #ManufacturerUsage;

    -- Create grouped usage by Manufacturer Only
    SELECT 
        m.Manufacturer,
        COUNT(pi.PrescriptionItemID) AS UsageCount,
        COUNT(DISTINCT p.PrescriptionID) AS PrescriptionCount
    INTO #ManufacturerUsage
    FROM Medications m
    INNER JOIN PrescriptionItems pi ON m.MedicationID = pi.MedicationID
    INNER JOIN Prescriptions p ON pi.PrescriptionID = p.PrescriptionID
    WHERE 
        m.IsDeleted = 0
        AND pi.IsDeleted = 0
        AND p.IsDeleted = 0
        AND (@CompanyName IS NULL OR m.Manufacturer = @CompanyName)
        AND (
            @SearchTerm IS NULL
            OR m.Manufacturer LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
        )
        AND (
            @DateFilter IS NULL
            OR (p.CreatedAt >= @StartDate AND p.CreatedAt < @EndDate)
        )
    GROUP BY m.Manufacturer;

    -- Total Count
    SELECT @TotalCount = COUNT(*) FROM #ManufacturerUsage;

    -- Paged Result
    SELECT 
        Manufacturer,
        UsageCount,
        PrescriptionCount
    FROM #ManufacturerUsage
    ORDER BY UsageCount DESC
    OFFSET (@PageNumber - 1) * @PageSize ROWS
    FETCH NEXT @PageSize ROWS ONLY;

    DROP TABLE #ManufacturerUsage;
END
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Patients_GetAgeDistribution]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetAgeDistribution]
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        CASE 
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 18 AND 25 THEN '18â€“25 years'
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 26 AND 40 THEN '26â€“40 years'
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 41 AND 55 THEN '41â€“55 years'
            WHEN PatientAge >= 56 THEN '56+ years'
            ELSE 'Unknown'
        END AS AgeGroup,
        COUNT(*) AS PatientCount
    FROM Patients
    WHERE IsDeleted = 0
    GROUP BY 
        CASE 
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 18 AND 25 THEN '18â€“25 years'
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 26 AND 40 THEN '26â€“40 years'
            WHEN PatientAge BETWEEN 41 AND 55 THEN '41â€“55 years'
            WHEN PatientAge >= 56 THEN '56+ years'
            ELSE 'Unknown'
        END
    ORDER BY AgeGroup;
END
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetAll]
    @PageNumber INT = 1,             -- Page number
    @PageSize   INT = 10,            -- Records per page
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = '',  -- Search filter
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
	@FollowupDate NVARCHAR(100) = ''
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    DECLARE @Offset INT = (@PageNumber - 1) * @PageSize;

    ;WITH PatientCTE AS
    (
        SELECT 
            p.PatientID,
            p.UserID,
            p.PatientReferenceID,
            p.DateOfBirth,
            p.Gender,
            p.Address,
            p.BloodGroup,
            p.InsuranceProvider,
            p.InsurancePolicyNumber,
            p.PatientAge,
			p.PatientCode,
            u.FirstName,
            u.LastName,
            u.Email,
            u.PhoneNumber,
			pr.FollowupDate,
            u.ReferenceUserId,
            COUNT(*) OVER() AS TotalCount
        FROM Patients p
        INNER JOIN Users u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
        -- Optional join to filter by DoctorID
        LEFT JOIN Prescriptions pr ON pr.PatientID = p.PatientID
        LEFT JOIN Doctors d ON d.DoctorID = pr.DoctorID
        WHERE p.IsDeleted = 0
          AND u.IsDeleted = 0
          AND (
                @SearchTerm = '' 
                OR u.FirstName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR u.LastName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR u.Email LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR u.PhoneNumber LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR p.PatientAge LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
                OR p.Gender LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%' 
				OR P.PatientCode LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
              )
          AND (@DoctorID IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID)
          AND (pr.IsDeleted = 0 OR pr.PrescriptionID IS NULL)
		  AND (@FollowupDate = '' OR CONVERT(DATE, pr.FollowUpDate) = CONVERT(DATE, @FollowupDate))
    )
    SELECT *
    FROM PatientCTE
    ORDER BY PatientID DESC
    OFFSET @Offset ROWS FETCH NEXT @PageSize ROWS ONLY;
END;
GO

        ISNULL(p.BloodGroup, '') AS BloodGroup,
        ISNULL(p.InsuranceProvider, '') AS InsuranceProvider,
        ISNULL(p.InsurancePolicyNumber, '') AS InsurancePolicyNumber,

        p.CreatedAt,
        p.UpdatedAt,
        p.IsDeleted,
        ISNULL(p.PatientCode, '') AS PatientCode,
        ISNULL(p.FullName, '') AS FullName,
        ISNULL(p.IsSelf, 0) AS IsSelf,
        ISNULL(p.PatientName, '') AS PatientName,
        ISNULL(p.Age, 0) AS Age,
        ISNULL(p.City, '') AS City,
        ISNULL(p.ZipCode, '') AS ZipCode,
        ISNULL(p.Country, '') AS Country,
        ISNULL(p.MobileNo, '') AS MobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.PatientMobileNo, '') AS PatientMobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.Email, '') AS Email,
        ISNULL(p.PatientEmail, '') AS PatientEmail,
        ISNULL(p.CreatedBy, '') AS CreatedBy,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorCode, '') AS CreatorCode,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorRole, '') AS CreatorRole,

        u.LastName,

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetByAgentMaster]
    @AgentMasterID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    -- This assumes there's a relationship between Patient and AgentMaster
    -- Adjust the query based on your actual schema
    SELECT DISTINCT
        p.[PatientID],
        p.[UserID],
        p.[DateOfBirth],
        p.[Gender],
        p.[Address],
        p.[BloodGroup],
        p.[InsuranceProvider],
        p.[InsurancePolicyNumber],
        p.[PatientAge],
        p.[CreatedAt],
        p.[UpdatedAt],
        p.[IsDeleted],
        p.[PatientReferenceID],
        p.[PatientCode]
    FROM [dbo].[Patient] p
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[MasterDoctor] md ON p.[PatientID] = md.[DoctorID]  -- Adjust join based on actual schema
    WHERE md.[AgentMasterID] = @AgentMasterID
        AND p.[IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY p.[CreatedAt] DESC;
    
    -- Alternative implementation if Patient has direct AgentMasterID relationship:
    -- WHERE p.[AgentMasterID] = @AgentMasterID AND p.[IsDeleted] = 0
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetByPhoneAndCode]
    @PatientCode NVARCHAR(100),
    @PhoneNo NVARCHAR(20)
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT TOP 1
        p.PatientID,
        p.UserID,
        p.PatientReferenceID,
        p.DateOfBirth,
        ISNULL(p.Gender, '') AS Gender,
        ISNULL(p.Address, '') AS Address,
        ISNULL(p.BloodGroup, '') AS BloodGroup,
        ISNULL(p.InsuranceProvider, '') AS InsuranceProvider,
        ISNULL(p.InsurancePolicyNumber, '') AS InsurancePolicyNumber,
        CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), ISNULL(p.PatientAge, 0)) AS PatientAge,
        p.CreatedAt,
        p.UpdatedAt,
        p.IsDeleted,
        ISNULL(p.PatientCode, '') AS PatientCode,
        ISNULL(p.FullName, '') AS FullName,
        ISNULL(p.IsSelf, 0) AS IsSelf,
        ISNULL(p.PatientName, '') AS PatientName,
        ISNULL(p.Age, 0) AS Age,
        ISNULL(p.City, '') AS City,
        ISNULL(p.ZipCode, '') AS ZipCode,
        ISNULL(p.Country, '') AS Country,
        ISNULL(p.MobileNo, '') AS MobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.PatientMobileNo, '') AS PatientMobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.Email, '') AS Email,
        ISNULL(p.PatientEmail, '') AS PatientEmail,
        ISNULL(p.CreatedBy, '') AS CreatedBy,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorCode, '') AS CreatorCode,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorRole, '') AS CreatorRole,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorEntityId, 0) AS CreatorEntityId,
        ISNULL(p.IsFirstTime, 0) AS IsFirstTime,
        u.PhoneNumber
    FROM Patients p
    LEFT JOIN Users u ON u.UserID = p.UserID
    WHERE (p.PatientCode = @PatientCode OR u.PhoneNumber = @PhoneNo)
        AND p.IsDeleted = 0;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetBySearchUserProfileId]
    @ProfileId INT,
    @Role NVARCHAR(50),
    @Name NVARCHAR(100) = ''
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        p.PatientID,
        p.UserID,
        p.PatientReferenceID,
        p.DateOfBirth,
        ISNULL(p.Gender, '') AS Gender,
        ISNULL(p.Address, '') AS Address,
        ISNULL(p.BloodGroup, '') AS BloodGroup,
        ISNULL(p.InsuranceProvider, '') AS InsuranceProvider,
        ISNULL(p.InsurancePolicyNumber, '') AS InsurancePolicyNumber,
        CONVERT(NVARCHAR(10), ISNULL(p.PatientAge, 0)) AS PatientAge,
        p.CreatedAt,
        p.UpdatedAt,
        p.IsDeleted,
        ISNULL(p.PatientCode, '') AS PatientCode,
        ISNULL(p.FullName, '') AS FullName,
        ISNULL(p.IsSelf, 0) AS IsSelf,
        ISNULL(p.PatientName, '') AS PatientName,
        ISNULL(p.Age, 0) AS Age,
        ISNULL(p.City, '') AS City,
        ISNULL(p.ZipCode, '') AS ZipCode,
        ISNULL(p.Country, '') AS Country,
        ISNULL(p.MobileNo, '') AS MobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.PatientMobileNo, '') AS PatientMobileNo,
        ISNULL(p.Email, '') AS Email,
        ISNULL(p.PatientEmail, '') AS PatientEmail,
        ISNULL(p.CreatedBy, '') AS CreatedBy,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorCode, '') AS CreatorCode,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorRole, '') AS CreatorRole,
        ISNULL(p.CreatorEntityId, 0) AS CreatorEntityId,
        ISNULL(p.IsFirstTime, 0) AS IsFirstTime,
        u.PhoneNumber,
        u.FirstName,
        u.LastName,
        u.Email AS UserEmail
    FROM Patients p
    LEFT JOIN Users u ON u.UserID = p.UserID
    WHERE p.CreatorEntityId = @ProfileId
        AND ISNULL(p.CreatorRole, '') = @Role
        AND (
            @Name = '' OR 
            ISNULL(p.FullName, '') LIKE '%' + @Name + '%' OR
            ISNULL(p.PatientName, '') LIKE '%' + @Name + '%' OR
            ISNULL(u.FirstName, '') + ' ' + ISNULL(u.LastName, '') LIKE '%' + @Name + '%'
        )
        AND p.IsDeleted = 0
    ORDER BY p.CreatedAt DESC;
END;

SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetCount]
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = '',
    @DoctorID INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT COUNT(*) AS TotalPatients
    FROM Patients p
    INNER JOIN Users u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    LEFT JOIN Prescriptions pr ON pr.PatientID = p.PatientID
    LEFT JOIN Doctors d ON d.DoctorID = pr.DoctorID
    WHERE p.IsDeleted = 0
      AND u.IsDeleted = 0
      AND (@SearchTerm = ''
           OR u.FirstName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
           OR u.LastName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
           OR u.Email LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
           OR u.PhoneNumber LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
           OR p.PatientAge LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
           OR p.Gender LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%')
      AND (@DoctorID IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID)
      AND (pr.IsDeleted = 0 OR pr.PrescriptionID IS NULL);
END;
GO
