/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Patients_GetFollowUpPatients]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetFollowUpPatients]
    @StartDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @EndDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        CONVERT(DATE, pr.FollowUpDate) AS FollowUpDate,
        p.PatientID,
        p.PatientCode,
        u.FirstName + ' ' + u.LastName AS PatientName,
        p.PatientReferenceID,
        u.PhoneNumber AS ContactNumber,
        pr.PrescriptionID,
        d.DoctorReferenceID,
        pr.IssueDate
    FROM Prescriptions pr
    INNER JOIN Patients p ON pr.PatientID = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN Users u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    LEFT JOIN Doctors d ON d.DoctorID = pr.DoctorID
    WHERE 
       pr.FollowUpDate IS NOT NULL
      -- Date range filter: if both are NULL, return all; otherwise apply range
      AND (
            (@StartDate IS NULL AND @EndDate IS NULL) 
            OR 
            (CONVERT(DATE, pr.FollowUpDate) BETWEEN CONVERT(DATE, @StartDate) AND CONVERT(DATE, @EndDate))
          )
      -- Doctor filter: if NULL, return all; otherwise filter by doctor
      AND (@DoctorID IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID)
    ORDER BY pr.FollowUpDate ASC;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Patients_GetFollowUpPatientsList]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE   PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetFollowUpPatientsList]
    @StartDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @EndDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    SELECT 
        p.PatientID,
        p.UserID,
        p.PatientReferenceID,
        p.DateOfBirth,
        p.Gender,
        p.Address,
        p.BloodGroup,
        p.InsuranceProvider,
        p.InsurancePolicyNumber,
        p.PatientAge,
        p.PatientCode,
        u.FirstName,
        u.LastName,
        u.Email,
        u.PhoneNumber as PhoneNumber,
        u.ReferenceUserId,
        pr.FollowUpDate,
        pr.IssueDate AS LastVisit,
        COUNT(*) OVER() AS TotalCount
    FROM Prescriptions pr
    INNER JOIN Patients p ON pr.PatientID = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN Users u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    LEFT JOIN Doctors d ON d.DoctorID = pr.DoctorID
    WHERE pr.IsDeleted = 0
      AND p.IsDeleted = 0
      AND u.IsDeleted = 0
      AND pr.FollowUpDate IS NOT NULL
      -- Date range filter: if both are NULL, return all; otherwise apply range
      AND (
            (@StartDate IS NULL AND @EndDate IS NULL) 
            OR 
            (CONVERT(DATE, pr.FollowUpDate) BETWEEN CONVERT(DATE, @StartDate) AND CONVERT(DATE, @EndDate))
          )
      -- Doctor filter: if NULL, return all; otherwise filter by doctor
      AND (@DoctorID IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID)
    ORDER BY pr.FollowUpDate ASC, u.FirstName ASC;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Patients_GetTotalCount]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_GetTotalCount]
    @SearchTerm NVARCHAR(100) = '',  
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
	@FollowupDate NVARCHAR(100) = ''
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT COUNT(*) AS TotalCount
    FROM Patients p
    INNER JOIN Users u ON p.UserID = u.UserID
    LEFT JOIN Prescriptions pr ON pr.PatientID = p.PatientID
    LEFT JOIN Doctors d ON d.DoctorID = pr.DoctorID
    WHERE p.IsDeleted = 0
      AND u.IsDeleted = 0
      AND (
            @SearchTerm = '' 
            OR u.FirstName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR u.LastName LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR u.Email LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR u.PhoneNumber LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR p.PatientAge LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
            OR p.Gender LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
			OR P.PatientCode LIKE '%' + @SearchTerm + '%'
          )
      AND (@DoctorID IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID)
      AND (pr.IsDeleted = 0 OR pr.PrescriptionID IS NULL)
	  AND (@FollowupDate = '' OR CONVERT(DATE, pr.FollowUpDate) = CONVERT(DATE, @FollowupDate));
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_Insert]
    @PatientID INT,
    @UserID INT,
	@PatientReferenceID INT = NULL,
    @DateOfBirth DATETIME = NULL,
    @Gender NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @Address NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @BloodGroup NVARCHAR(5) = NULL,
    @InsuranceProvider NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @InsurancePolicyNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
	@PatientAge NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
	@PatientCode NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @FullName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @IsSelf BIT = NULL,
    @PatientName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Age INT = NULL,
    @City NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @ZipCode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @Country NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @MobileNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @PatientMobileNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @Email NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @PatientEmail NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @CreatedBy NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CreatorCode NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CreatorRole NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CreatorEntityId INT = NULL,
    @IsFirstTime BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO Patients (
        UserID,
		PatientReferenceID,
        DateOfBirth,
        Gender,
        Address,
        BloodGroup,
        InsuranceProvider,
        InsurancePolicyNumber,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
		PatientAge,
		PatientCode,
        FullName,
        IsSelf,
        PatientName,
        Age,
        City,
        ZipCode,
        Country,
        MobileNo,
        PatientMobileNo,
        Email,
        PatientEmail,
        CreatedBy,
        CreatorCode,
        CreatorRole,
        CreatorEntityId,
        IsFirstTime
    )
    VALUES (
        @UserID,
		@PatientReferenceID,
        @DateOfBirth,
        ISNULL(@Gender, ''),
        ISNULL(@Address, ''),
        ISNULL(@BloodGroup, ''),
        ISNULL(@InsuranceProvider, ''),
        ISNULL(@InsurancePolicyNumber, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0,
		ISNULL(@PatientAge, ''),
		ISNULL(@PatientCode, ''),
        ISNULL(@FullName, ''),
        @IsSelf,
        ISNULL(@PatientName, ''),
        @Age,
        ISNULL(@City, ''),
        ISNULL(@ZipCode, ''),
        ISNULL(@Country, ''),
        ISNULL(@MobileNo, ''),
        ISNULL(@PatientMobileNo, ''),
        ISNULL(@Email, ''),
        ISNULL(@PatientEmail, ''),
        ISNULL(@CreatedBy, ''),
        ISNULL(@CreatorCode, ''),
        ISNULL(@CreatorRole, ''),
        @CreatorEntityId,
        @IsFirstTime
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PatientID;
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Patients_Update] 
    @PatientID INT,
	@PatientReferenceID INT = NULL,
    @UserID INT = NULL,
    @DateOfBirth DATETIME = NULL,
    @Gender NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @Address NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @BloodGroup NVARCHAR(5) = NULL,
    @InsuranceProvider NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @InsurancePolicyNumber NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @PatientAge NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @PatientCode NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @FullName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @IsSelf BIT = NULL,
    @PatientName NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @Age INT = NULL,
    @City NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @ZipCode NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @Country NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @MobileNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @PatientMobileNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @Email NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @PatientEmail NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @CreatedBy NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CreatorCode NVARCHAR(100) = NULL,
    @CreatorRole NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @CreatorEntityId INT = NULL,
    @IsFirstTime BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if PatientID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Patients
    SET 
        UserID = CASE WHEN @UserID > 0 THEN @UserID ELSE UserID END,
		PatientReferenceID = CASE WHEN @PatientReferenceID > 0 THEN @PatientReferenceID ELSE PatientReferenceID END,
        DateOfBirth = ISNULL(@DateOfBirth, DateOfBirth),
        Gender = ISNULL(@Gender, Gender),
        Address = ISNULL(@Address, Address),
        BloodGroup = ISNULL(@BloodGroup, BloodGroup),
        InsuranceProvider = ISNULL(@InsuranceProvider, InsuranceProvider),
        InsurancePolicyNumber = ISNULL(@InsurancePolicyNumber, InsurancePolicyNumber),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE(),
		PatientAge = ISNULL(@PatientAge, PatientAge),
        PatientCode = ISNULL(@PatientCode, PatientCode),
        FullName = ISNULL(@FullName, FullName),
        IsSelf = ISNULL(@IsSelf, IsSelf),
        PatientName = ISNULL(@PatientName, PatientName),
        Age = ISNULL(@Age, Age),
        City = ISNULL(@City, City),
        ZipCode = ISNULL(@ZipCode, ZipCode),
        Country = ISNULL(@Country, Country),
        MobileNo = ISNULL(@MobileNo, MobileNo),
        PatientMobileNo = ISNULL(@PatientMobileNo, PatientMobileNo),
        Email = ISNULL(@Email, Email),
        PatientEmail = ISNULL(@PatientEmail, PatientEmail),
        CreatedBy = ISNULL(@CreatedBy, CreatedBy),
        CreatorCode = ISNULL(@CreatorCode, CreatorCode),
        CreatorRole = ISNULL(@CreatorRole, CreatorRole),
        CreatorEntityId = ISNULL(@CreatorEntityId, CreatorEntityId),
        IsFirstTime = ISNULL(@IsFirstTime, IsFirstTime)
    WHERE 
        PatientID = @PatientID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PatientID AS UpdatedPatientID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPatientID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

    @Id INT,
    @DisplayName NVARCHAR(150),
    @PermissionValue NVARCHAR(500),
    @UpdatedId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Pharmacy_DeleteById] 
    @PharmacyID INT -- Corrected parameter name and added @ symbol
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Soft delete the pharmacy by setting IsDeleted to 1
    UPDATE Pharmacies
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        PharmacyId = @PharmacyID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PharmacyID AS UpdatedPharmacyID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPharmacyID;
    END
END;
GO

    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL

    BEGIN
        SELECT @PharmacyID AS UpdatedPharmacyID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPharmacyID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Prescription_GetAnaytics]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Prescription_GetAnaytics]
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        SUM(CASE WHEN p.Gender = 'Male' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Male,
        SUM(CASE WHEN p.Gender = 'Female' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS Female,
        COUNT(pp.PrescriptionPdfID) AS TotalPrescription
    FROM PrescriptionPdf pp
    INNER JOIN Patients p
        ON pp.PatientID = p.PatientID
    WHERE pp.IsDeleted = 0
      AND p.IsDeleted = 0;
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Prescription_Insert]
    @PrescriptionId INT,
    @TenantId INT,
    @PatientId INT,
    @DoctorId INT,
	@IsHeader BIT=0,
	@IsPreHand BIT=0,
    @PatientFollowUpId INT = NULL,
    @PharmacyId INT = NULL,
    @IssueDate DATETIME,
    @ExpiryDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @Language NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @StatusId INT,
    @FollowUpDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsArchived BIT = 0,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = 0,
	@AppointmentRefId INT=NULL,
	@PrescriptionCode NVARCHAR(100)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO [dbo].[Prescriptions] (
        TenantId,
        PatientId,
        DoctorId,
        PatientFollowUpId,
        PharmacyId,
        IssueDate,
        ExpiryDate,
        Language,
        StatusId,
        FollowUpDate,
        IsArchived,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
		IsHeader,
		AppointmentRefId,
		isPreHand,
		PrescriptionCode
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantId,
        @PatientId,
        @DoctorId,
        @PatientFollowUpId,
        @PharmacyId,
        @IssueDate,
        @ExpiryDate,
        @Language,
        @StatusId,
        @FollowUpDate,
        @IsArchived,
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        @IsDeleted,
		@IsHeader,
		@AppointmentRefId,
		@IsPreHand,
		@PrescriptionCode
    );
    
    -- Return the newly inserted Id as Id
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PrescriptionId;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Prescription_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Prescription_Update] 
    @PrescriptionId INT,
    @TenantId INT = NULL,
    @PatientId INT = NULL,
    @DoctorId INT = NULL,
    @PatientFollowUpId INT = NULL,
    @PharmacyId INT = NULL,
    @IssueDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @ExpiryDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @Language NVARCHAR(10) = NULL,
    @StatusId INT = NULL,
    @FollowUpDate DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsArchived BIT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
   @IsHeader BIT=0,
   @AppointmentRefId INT=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if PrescriptionId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Prescriptions
    SET 
        TenantId = CASE WHEN @TenantId > 0 THEN @TenantId ELSE TenantId END,
        PatientId = CASE WHEN @PatientId > 0 THEN @PatientId ELSE PatientId END,
        DoctorId = CASE WHEN @DoctorId > 0 THEN @DoctorId ELSE DoctorId END,
        PatientFollowUpId = CASE WHEN @PatientFollowUpId > 0 THEN @PatientFollowUpId ELSE PatientFollowUpId END,
        PharmacyId = CASE WHEN @PharmacyId > 0 THEN @PharmacyId ELSE PharmacyId END,
        IssueDate = ISNULL(@IssueDate, IssueDate),
        ExpiryDate = @ExpiryDate,
        Language = ISNULL(@Language, Language),
        StatusId = CASE WHEN @StatusId > 0 THEN @StatusId ELSE StatusId END,
		AppointmentRefId=CASE WHEN @AppointmentRefId>0 THEN @AppointmentRefId ELSE AppointmentRefId END,
        FollowUpDate = @FollowUpDate,
        IsArchived = ISNULL(@IsArchived, IsArchived),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE(),
		IsHeader=@IsHeader
    WHERE 
        PrescriptionId = @PrescriptionId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PrescriptionId AS UpdatedPrescriptionId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPrescriptionId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionAdvice_GetByPrescriptionId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionAdvice_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        ca.Advice AS Name,
        pa.Description AS Notes,
        pa.CommonAdviceID AS Id
    FROM 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[PrescriptionAdvice] pa
    JOIN 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[CommonAdvices] ca 
        ON pa.CommonAdviceID = ca.CommonAdviceID
    WHERE 
        pa.PrescriptionID = @PrescriptionId
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionAdvice_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionAdvice_Update] 
    @PrescriptionAdviceId INT,
    @PrescriptionId INT = NULL,
    @CommonAdviceId INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
	@CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@IsActive BIT=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if AdviceId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE PrescriptionAdvice
    SET 
        PrescriptionId = CASE WHEN @PrescriptionID > 0 THEN @PrescriptionId ELSE PrescriptionId END,
        CommonAdviceId=CASE WHEN @CommonAdviceId > 0 THEN @CommonAdviceId ELSE CommonAdviceId END,
          Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),
		UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        PrescriptionAdviceId = @PrescriptionAdviceId
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PrescriptionAdviceId AS UpdatedPrescriptionAdviceId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPrescriptionAdviceId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionDiagnoses_GetById]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        pd.PrescriptionDiagnosisId ,
         pd.PrescriptionId,
        pd.DiagnosisID AS Id,
        ISNULL(pd.Notes, '') AS Notes,
		ISNULL(d.Name, '') AS DiagnosisName,
		PastDiagnosis,
		PresentDiagnosis,
        pd.CreatedAt,
        pd.UpdatedAt,
        pd.IsDeleted
    FROM PrescriptionDiagnoses pd
	 JOIN 
        Diagonosis d ON pd.DiagnosisID = d.DiagonosisID

    WHERE PrescriptionId = @PrescriptionId ;
END;
GO

	@PastDiagnosis NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@PresentDiagnosis  NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,

    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL

		PresentDiagnosis,

    );

END;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionDiagnoses_Update] 
    @PrescriptionDiagnosisId INT,
    @PrescriptionId INT = NULL,
    @DiagnosisId INT = NULL,
    @Notes NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if PrescriptionDiagnosisId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE PrescriptionDiagnoses
    SET 
        PrescriptionId = CASE WHEN @PrescriptionId > 0 THEN @PrescriptionId ELSE PrescriptionId END,
        DiagnosisId = CASE WHEN @DiagnosisId > 0 THEN @DiagnosisId ELSE DiagnosisId END,
        Notes = ISNULL(@Notes, Notes),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        PrescriptionDiagnosisId = @PrescriptionDiagnosisId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PrescriptionDiagnosisId AS UpdatedPrescriptionDiagnosisID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPrescriptionDiagnosisID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionExamination_GetByPrescriptionId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionExamination_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        -- Split the BloodPressure into Diastolic and Systolic and return as strings
        SUBSTRING(e.BloodPressure, 1, CHARINDEX('/', e.BloodPressure) - 1) AS Diastolic, 
        SUBSTRING(e.BloodPressure, CHARINDEX('/', e.BloodPressure) + 1, LEN(e.BloodPressure)) AS Systolic, 

        -- Return Pulse and Temperature as strings
        e.Pulse AS Pulse, 
        e.Temperature AS Temperature, 

        -- Return Findings as a string (if null, return 'not found')
        ISNULL(e.Findings, 'not found') AS Findings, 

        -- Extract and return HeightFeet, HeightInches, and Weight from Notes
        -- Assuming the format of Notes is 'HeightFeet+HeightInches+Weight'
        -- Extract HeightFeet
        CASE
            WHEN CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) > 0 THEN
                SUBSTRING(e.Notes, 1, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) - 1)
            ELSE ''
        END AS HeightFeet, 

        -- Extract HeightInches
        CASE
            WHEN CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) > 0 AND CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) + 1) > 0 THEN
                SUBSTRING(e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) + 1, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) + 1) - CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) - 1)
            ELSE ''
        END AS HeightInches,

        -- Extract Weight (after the second '+')
        CASE
            WHEN CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) + 1) > 0 THEN
                SUBSTRING(e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes, CHARINDEX('+', e.Notes) + 1) + 1, LEN(e.Notes))
            ELSE ''
        END AS Weight,


        -- Static value for TenantId as a string
        '1' AS TenantId,

        -- Format the Examination Date to today as a string
        CAST(GETDATE() AS DATE) AS ExaminationDate

    FROM 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[PrescriptionExaminations] pe
    JOIN 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[Examinations] e 
        ON pe.ExaminationID = e.ExaminationID
    WHERE 
        pe.PrescriptionId = @PrescriptionId
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionInvestigation_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        i.Name,
        pi.Description AS Notes,
        pi.InvestigationID AS Id
    FROM 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[PrescriptionInvestigation] pi
    JOIN 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[Investigations] i 
        ON pi.InvestigationID = i.InvestigationID
    WHERE 
        pi.PrescriptionId = @PrescriptionId
        AND pi.IsDeleted = 0 
        AND i.IsDeleted = 0;
END;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionItem_Insert]
    @PrescriptionItemId INT,
    @PrescriptionId INT,
    @MedicationId INT,
    @Dosage NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Quantity INT,
	@Duration NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Timing  NVARCHAR(50)=NULL,
    @Instructions NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
	@MealTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    INSERT INTO PrescriptionItems (
        PrescriptionId,
        MedicationId,
        Dosage,
		Duration,
        Quantity,
		Timing,
        Instructions,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
		MealTime 
    )
    VALUES (
        @PrescriptionId,
        @MedicationId,
        ISNULL(@Dosage, ''),
		ISNULL(@Duration,''),
		ISNULL(@Timing,''),
        @Quantity,
        ISNULL(@Instructions, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0,
		ISNULL(@MealTime ,'')
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PrescriptionItemId;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionItem_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/

GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionItem_Update] 
    @PrescriptionItemId INT,
    @PrescriptionId INT = NULL,
    @MedicationId INT = NULL,
    @Dosage NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Duration NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@Timing NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
	@MealTime NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @Quantity INT = NULL,
    @Instructions NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if PrescriptionItemId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE PrescriptionItems
    SET 
        PrescriptionId = CASE WHEN @PrescriptionId > 0 THEN @PrescriptionId ELSE PrescriptionId END,
        MedicationId = CASE WHEN @MedicationId > 0 THEN @MedicationId ELSE MedicationId END,
        Dosage = ISNULL(@Dosage, Dosage),
        Quantity = ISNULL(@Quantity, Quantity),
		Timing=ISNULL(@Timing,Timing),
		MealTime=ISNULL(@MealTime,MealTime),
		Duration=ISNULL(@Duration,Duration),
        Instructions = ISNULL(@Instructions, Instructions),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        PrescriptionItemId = @PrescriptionItemId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PrescriptionItemId AS UpdatedPrescriptionItemId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPrescriptionItemId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionItems_GetByPrescriptionId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionItems_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
         CASE 
                WHEN ISNULL(DosageForm, '') <> '' 
                    THEN CONCAT(LEFT(DosageForm, 3), '. ', MedicationName, ' ', Strength)
                ELSE CONCAT(MedicationName, ' ', Strength)
            END AS Name,
        pi.Instructions AS Notes,
        pi.MedicationID AS Id,
        pi.Duration,
        pi.Dosage AS Timming,
        pi.MealTime
    FROM 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[Medications] m
    JOIN 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[PrescriptionItems] pi 
        ON m.MedicationID = pi.MedicationID
    WHERE 
        pi.PrescriptionID = @PrescriptionId AND pi.IsDeleted = 0;
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory_GetByName]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory_GetByName]
    @CommonHistoryName NVARCHAR(250) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    IF @CommonHistoryName IS NOT NULL
    BEGIN
        SELECT 
            PrescriptionPatientHistoryID,
            CommonHistoryID,
            PrescriptionID,
			Description,
            CreatedAt,
            UpdatedAt,
            IsDeleted
        FROM [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory]
        WHERE CommonHistoryID IN (
            SELECT CommonHistoryID 
            FROM [dbo].[CommonHistory]
            WHERE Name LIKE '%' + @CommonHistoryName + '%' AND IsDeleted = 0
        ) AND IsDeleted = 0;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT TOP 100
            PrescriptionPatientHistoryID,
            CommonHistoryID,
            PrescriptionID,
			Description,
            CreatedAt,
            UpdatedAt,
            IsDeleted
        FROM [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory]
        WHERE IsDeleted = 0 
        ORDER BY CreatedAt DESC;
    END
END;
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT TOP (1000)
        ch.Name,
        pp.PastHistory AS Notes,
        pp.PastHistory,
        pp.PresentHistory,
        pp.CommonHistoryId AS Id
    FROM 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory] pp
    JOIN 
        [SoowGood_System].[dbo].[CommonHistory] ch 
        ON pp.CommonHistoryID = ch.CommonHistoryId
    WHERE 
        pp.PrescriptionID = @PrescriptionId ;
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory_Insert]
    @PrescriptionPatientHistoryID INT,
    @CommonHistoryID INT,
    @PrescriptionID INT,
	@Description NVARCHAR(250)=NULL,
	@PresentHistory NVARCHAR(250)=NULL,
    @PastHistory NVARCHAR(250)=NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @IsActive BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    
    INSERT INTO PrescriptionPatientHistory (
        CommonHistoryID,
        PrescriptionID,
		Description,
		PastHistory,
		PresentHistory,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
        IsActive
    )
    VALUES (
        @CommonHistoryID,
        @PrescriptionID,
		ISNULL(@Description, ''),
		ISNULL(@PastHistory, ''),
		ISNULL(@PresentHistory, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0,
        1
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PrescriptionPatientHistoryID;
END;
GO

SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPatientHistory_Previous]
    @PatientID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT  
        ch.CommonHistoryId, 
        ch.Name, 
        ch.Description
    FROM Prescriptions p
    JOIN PrescriptionPatientHistory pph 
        ON p.PrescriptionID = pph.PrescriptionID
    JOIN CommonHistory ch 
        ON pph.CommonHistoryID = ch.CommonHistoryId
    WHERE p.PatientID = @PatientID 
      AND p.IsDeleted = 0;
END;
GO

    BEGIN

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_GetByAppointmentId]
    @appointmentId INT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT TOP 1
        PrescriptionPdfID,
        TenantID,
        PrescriptionID,
        DoctorID,
        PatientID,
        ISNULL(FilePath, '') AS FilePath,
        ISNULL(Description, '') AS Description,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted,
        IsActive,
        AppointmentRefId
    FROM PrescriptionPdf
    WHERE AppointmentRefId = @appointmentId AND IsDeleted = 0
    ORDER BY CreatedAt DESC; -- Sorting in descending order to get the most recent record
END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_GetByPatientDoctorId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_GetByPatientDoctorId]
   @patientId INT = NULL,
   @doctorId INT=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        pp.PrescriptionPdfID,
        pp.TenantID,
        pp.PrescriptionID,
        pp.FilePath,
        pp.Description,
        pp.CreatedAt,
        pp.UpdatedAt,
        pp.AppointmentRefId,
        d.UserId AS DoctorUserId,
        u1.FirstName  AS DoctorName,
        p.UserId AS PatientUserId,
        u2.FirstName  AS PatientName,
		pr.FollowUpDate

    FROM [PrescriptionPdf] AS pp
    INNER JOIN [Doctors] AS d ON pp.DoctorID = d.DoctorID
    INNER JOIN [Users] AS u1 ON d.UserId = u1.UserId

    INNER JOIN [Patients] AS p ON pp.PatientID = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN [Users] AS u2 ON p.UserId = u2.UserId
	LEFT JOIN [Prescriptions] AS pr
        ON pp.PrescriptionID = pr.PrescriptionID

    WHERE (@DoctorId=0 OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @doctorId)
      AND (@PatientId =0 OR p.PatientID = @patientId)
      
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_GetPrehandByDoctorId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_GetPrehandByDoctorId]
    @DoctorId INT = NULL,
	@PrescriptionCode NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @PatientName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @PatientCode NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        pp.PrescriptionPdfID,
        pp.TenantID,
        pp.PrescriptionID,
        pp.FilePath,
        pp.Description,
        pp.CreatedAt,
        pp.UpdatedAt,
        pp.AppointmentRefId,
        d.UserId AS DoctorUserId,
        u1.FirstName AS DoctorName,
        p.UserId AS PatientUserId,
        u2.FirstName AS PatientName,
        pr.FollowUpDate,
		pr.PrescriptionCode

    FROM PrescriptionPdf AS pp
    INNER JOIN Doctors AS d ON pp.DoctorID = d.DoctorID
    INNER JOIN Users AS u1 ON d.UserId = u1.UserId
    INNER JOIN Patients AS p ON pp.PatientID = p.PatientID
    INNER JOIN Users AS u2 ON p.UserId = u2.UserId
    LEFT JOIN Prescriptions AS pr ON pp.PrescriptionID = pr.PrescriptionID

   WHERE pr.isPreHand = 1
  AND (
        (@DoctorId IS NULL OR d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorId)
    )
  AND (@PrescriptionCode IS NULL OR pr.PrescriptionCode LIKE '%' + @PrescriptionCode + '%')
  AND (@PatientName IS NULL OR u2.FirstName LIKE '%' + @PatientName + '%')
  AND (@PatientCode IS NULL OR p.PatientCode LIKE '%' + @PatientCode + '%')

END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionPdf_Update]
    @PrescriptionPdfID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @PrescriptionID INT = NULL,
    @DoctorID INT = NULL,
    @PatientID INT = NULL,
    @FilePath NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
	@Description NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
	@IsActive BIT=NULL,
	@AppointmentRefId INT=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if PrescriptionPdfID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE PrescriptionPdf
    SET
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        PrescriptionID = CASE WHEN @PrescriptionID IS NOT NULL THEN @PrescriptionID ELSE PrescriptionID END,
        DoctorID = CASE WHEN @DoctorID IS NOT NULL THEN @DoctorID ELSE DoctorID END,
        PatientID = CASE WHEN @PatientID IS NOT NULL THEN @PatientID ELSE PatientID END,
        AppointmentRefId = CASE WHEN @AppointmentRefId IS NOT NULL THEN @AppointmentRefId ELSE AppointmentRefId END,
		
        FilePath = ISNULL(@FilePath, FilePath),
		Description = ISNULL(@Description, Description),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE
        PrescriptionPdfID = @PrescriptionPdfID
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @PrescriptionPdfID AS UpdatedPrescriptionPdfID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedPrescriptionPdfID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionSymptom_GetByPrescriptionId]
    @PrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

        ps.Duration,
        ps.Days,

        ps.PrescriptionID = @PrescriptionId 
END;

    @Duration NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,

    )

        ISNULL(@Days, ''),
        ISNULL(@Duration, ''),
		ISNULL(@Description, ''),

        1
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PrescriptionSymtomID;

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplate_Insert]
    @Name NVARCHAR(100),
    @PrescriptionID INT,
	@PrescriptionTemplateID INT,
    @DoctorID INT,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @IsDeleted BIT = 0,
	@Description NVARCHAR=NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;



    INSERT INTO [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplate] (
        [Name],
        PrescriptionID,
        DoctorID,
        IsActive,
        IsDeleted,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt
    )
    VALUES (
        @Name,
        @PrescriptionID,
        @DoctorID,
        @IsActive,
        @IsDeleted,
       GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE()
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS PrescriptionTemplateID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplate_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplate_Update]
    @PrescriptionTemplateID INT,
    @Name NVARCHAR(100),
    @PrescriptionID INT,
    @DoctorID INT,
    @IsActive BIT = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME
AS
BEGIN
    UPDATE PrescriptionTemplate
    SET
        Name = @Name,
        PrescriptionID = @PrescriptionID,
        DoctorID = @DoctorID,
        IsActive = @IsActive,
        IsDeleted = @IsDeleted,
        UpdatedAt = @UpdatedAt
    WHERE PrescriptionTemplateID = @PrescriptionTemplateID;
END;

AS

    FROM PrescriptionTemplate
    WHERE IsDeleted IS NULL OR IsDeleted = 0;

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplates_GetByDoctorId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[PrescriptionTemplates_GetByDoctorId]
   @DoctorID INT,
   @TemplateName NVARCHAR(50)=NULL
AS
BEGIN
    -- Retrieve prescription templates where the DoctorID is matched from the Doctors table based on DoctorReferenceID
    SELECT pt.*
    FROM PrescriptionTemplate pt
    JOIN Doctors d ON pt.DoctorID = d.DoctorID
    WHERE d.DoctorReferenceID = @DoctorID
    AND (pt.IsDeleted IS NULL OR pt.IsDeleted = 0)
  AND (@TemplateName IS NULL OR pt.Name LIKE '%' + @TemplateName + '%');
END;
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_DeleteById] 
    @ScannedPrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ScannedPrescriptionId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE ScannedPrescriptions
    SET 
        IsDeleted = 1,
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ScannedPrescriptionId = @ScannedPrescriptionId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ScannedPrescriptionId AS UpdatedScannedPrescriptionId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedScannedPrescriptionId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_GetAll]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

        CreatedAt,

    FROM ScannedPrescriptions
    WHERE IsDeleted = 0;
END;

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_GetById]
    @ScannedPrescriptionId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        ScannedPrescriptionId,
        TenantId,
        PrescriptionId,
        ISNULL(FilePath, '') AS FilePath,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    FROM ScannedPrescriptions
    WHERE ScannedPrescriptionId = @ScannedPrescriptionId AND IsDeleted = 0;
END;
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_Insert]
    @ScannedPrescriptionId INT,
    @TenantId INT,
    @PrescriptionId INT = NULL,
    @FilePath NVARCHAR(255),
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    INSERT INTO ScannedPrescriptions(
        TenantId,
        PrescriptionId,
        FilePath,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantId,
        @PrescriptionId,
        @FilePath,
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS ScannedPrescriptionId;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ScannedPrescription_Update] 
    @ScannedPrescriptionId INT,
    @TenantId INT = NULL,
    @PrescriptionId INT = NULL,
    @FilePath NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ScannedPrescriptionId exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE ScannedPrescriptions
    SET 
        TenantId = CASE WHEN @TenantId > 0 THEN @TenantId ELSE TenantId END,
        PrescriptionId = CASE WHEN @PrescriptionId > 0 THEN @PrescriptionId ELSE PrescriptionId END,
        FilePath = ISNULL(@FilePath, FilePath),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ScannedPrescriptionId = @ScannedPrescriptionId 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ScannedPrescriptionId AS UpdatedScannedPrescriptionId;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedScannedPrescriptionId;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

SET ANSI_NULLS ON

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Schedule_Insert]
    @ScheduleID INT,
    @TenantID INT,
    @Day NVARCHAR(20),
    @Time NVARCHAR(5),
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    INSERT INTO Schedule(
        TenantID,
        Day,
        Time,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsDeleted
    )
    VALUES (
        @TenantID,
		ISNULL(@Day, ''),
		ISNULL(@Time, ''),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        GETUTCDATE(),
        0
    );

    -- Return the newly inserted ScheduleID
    SELECT SCOPE_IDENTITY() AS ScheduleID;
END;
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Schedule_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Schedule_Update] 
    @ScheduleID INT,
    @TenantID INT = NULL,
    @Day NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @Time NVARCHAR(5) = NULL,
    @UpdatedAt DATETIME = NULL,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @CreatedAt DATETIME = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    -- Update only if ScheduleID exists and is not deleted
    UPDATE Schedule
    SET 
        TenantID = CASE WHEN @TenantID > 0 THEN @TenantID ELSE TenantID END,
        Day = ISNULL(@Day, Day),
        Time = ISNULL(@Time, Time),
        UpdatedAt = GETUTCDATE()
    WHERE 
        ScheduleID = @ScheduleID 
        AND IsDeleted = 0;

    -- Check if the update was successful
    IF @@ROWCOUNT > 0
    BEGIN
        SELECT @ScheduleID AS UpdatedScheduleID;
    END
    ELSE
    BEGIN
        SELECT NULL AS UpdatedScheduleID;  -- Return NULL if no update happened
    END
END;
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Symptom_GetSymptomsByName]
    @SymptomName NVARCHAR(100) = NULL
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [SymptomID],
        [TenantID],
        [SymptomName],
        [Description],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[Symptoms]
    WHERE ([SymptomName] LIKE '%' + @SymptomName + '%' OR @SymptomName IS NULL)
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[User_GetAll]
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [UserID],
        [TenantID],
        [FirstName],
        [LastName],
        [FullName],
        [UserName],
        [Email],
        [PasswordHash],
        [UserType],

        [ContactNo],
        [RoleId],

        [IsDeleted],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [ReferenceUserId]
    FROM [dbo].[Users]
    WHERE [IsDeleted] = 0 OR [IsDeleted] IS NULL
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[User_GetByreferenceId]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[User_GetByreferenceId]
    @ReferenceUserId INT,
	@userType Nvarchar(255),
	@TenantId INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;

    SELECT 
        UserID,
        TenantID,
        FirstName,
		ISNULL(FirstName, '') AS FirstName,
		ISNULL(LastName, '') AS LastName,
		ISNULL(Email, '') AS Email,
		ISNULL(PasswordHash, '') AS PasswordHash,
		ISNULL(UserType, '') AS UserType,
		ISNULL(PhoneNumber, '') AS PhoneNumber,
        CreatedAt,
        UpdatedAt,
        IsActive,
        IsDeleted,
        ReferenceUserId
    FROM Users
    WHERE ReferenceUserId = @ReferenceUserId AND UserType=@userType AND TenantID=@TenantId AND IsDeleted = 0;

END;
GO

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[User_Insert]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[User_Insert]
    @TenantID INT,
    @FirstName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @LastName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @FullName NVARCHAR(150),
    @UserName NVARCHAR(100),
    @Email NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
    @PasswordHash NVARCHAR(255),
    @UserType NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @PhoneNumber NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
    @ContactNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @RoleId INT,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @ReferenceUserId INT = NULL,
    @UserID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    INSERT INTO [dbo].[Users]
    (
        [TenantID],
        [FirstName],
        [LastName],
        [FullName],
        [UserName],
        [Email],
        [PasswordHash],
        [UserType],
        [PhoneNumber],
        [ContactNo],
        [RoleId],
        [IsActive],
        [IsDeleted],
        [CreatedAt],
        [ReferenceUserId]
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @TenantID,
        @FirstName,
        @LastName,
        @FullName,
        @UserName,
        @Email,
        @PasswordHash,
        @UserType,
        @PhoneNumber,
        @ContactNo,
        @RoleId,
        @IsActive,
        0,
        GETDATE(),
        @ReferenceUserId
    );
    SET @UserID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[User_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[User_Update]
    @UserID INT,
    @TenantID INT,
    @FirstName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @LastName NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
    @FullName NVARCHAR(150),
    @UserName NVARCHAR(100),
    @Email NVARCHAR(150) = NULL,
    @PasswordHash NVARCHAR(255) = NULL,
    @UserType NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @PhoneNumber NVARCHAR(15) = NULL,
    @ContactNo NVARCHAR(20) = NULL,
    @RoleId INT,
    @IsActive BIT = 1,
    @IsDeleted BIT = NULL,
    @ReferenceUserId INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedId INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[Users]
    SET 
        [TenantID] = @TenantID,
        [FirstName] = @FirstName,
        [LastName] = @LastName,
        [FullName] = @FullName,
        [UserName] = @UserName,
        [Email] = @Email,
        [PasswordHash] = ISNULL(@PasswordHash, [PasswordHash]),
        [UserType] = @UserType,
        [PhoneNumber] = @PhoneNumber,
        [ContactNo] = @ContactNo,
        [RoleId] = @RoleId,
        [IsActive] = @IsActive,
        [IsDeleted] = ISNULL(@IsDeleted, [IsDeleted]),
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE(),
        [ReferenceUserId] = @ReferenceUserId
    WHERE [UserID] = @UserID;
    SET @UpdatedId = @UserID;
END
GO

AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT 
        [SpecializationID],
        [SpecialityID],
        [SpecializationName],
        [Description],
        [TenantID],
        [CreatedAt],
        [UpdatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[Specialization]
    WHERE [IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY [CreatedAt] DESC;
END

/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Specialization_GetFiltered]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[Specialization_GetFiltered]') AND type in (N'P', N'PC'))
    DROP PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_GetFiltered]
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_GetFiltered]
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    SELECT DISTINCT
        s.[SpecializationID],
        s.[SpecialityID],
        s.[SpecializationName],
        s.[Description],
        s.[TenantID],
        s.[CreatedAt],
        s.[UpdatedAt],
        s.[IsDeleted]
    FROM [dbo].[Specialization] s
    INNER JOIN [dbo].[DoctorSpecialization] ds ON s.[SpecializationID] = ds.[SpecializationID]
    WHERE s.[IsDeleted] = 0
        AND ds.[IsDeleted] = 0
    ORDER BY s.[CreatedAt] DESC;
END
GO

CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_Insert]
    @SpecializationName NVARCHAR(200),
    @Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @SpecialityID INT = NULL,
    @TenantID INT,
    @SpecializationID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    INSERT INTO [dbo].[Specialization]
    (
        [SpecializationName],
        [Description],
        [SpecialityID],
        [TenantID],
        [CreatedAt],
        [IsDeleted]
    )
    VALUES
    (
        @SpecializationName,
        @Description,
        @SpecialityID,
        @TenantID,
        GETDATE(),
        0
    );
    SET @SpecializationID = SCOPE_IDENTITY();
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Specialization_Update]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[Specialization_Update]') AND type in (N'P', N'PC'))
    DROP PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_Update]
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_Update]
    @SpecializationID INT,
    @SpecializationName NVARCHAR(200) = NULL,
    @Description NVARCHAR(500) = NULL,
    @SpecialityID INT = NULL,
    @UpdatedSpecializationID INT OUTPUT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[Specialization]
    SET 
        [SpecializationName] = ISNULL(@SpecializationName, [SpecializationName]),
        [Description] = ISNULL(@Description, [Description]),
        [SpecialityID] = @SpecialityID,
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE()
    WHERE [SpecializationID] = @SpecializationID
        AND [IsDeleted] = 0;
    
    SET @UpdatedSpecializationID = @SpecializationID;
END
GO
/****** Object:  StoredProcedure [dbo].[Specialization_DeleteById]    Script Date: 2/8/2026 6:49:45 AM ******/
SET ANSI_NULLS ON
GO
SET QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON
GO
IF EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[Specialization_DeleteById]') AND type in (N'P', N'PC'))
    DROP PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_DeleteById]
GO
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[Specialization_DeleteById]
    @SpecializationID INT
AS
BEGIN
    SET NOCOUNT ON;
    UPDATE [dbo].[Specialization]
    SET 
        [IsDeleted] = 1,
        [UpdatedAt] = GETDATE()
    WHERE [SpecializationID] = @SpecializationID;
END
